<script type="85cd9de20ab38d2dba3dc7e1-text/javascript">
    function togglePrivacyMode() {
        var balanceElement = document.getElementById('balanceShowHeader1');
        if (!balanceElement) return;

        var isBlurred = balanceElement.classList.contains('blurred-balance');

        if (isBlurred) {
            balanceElement.classList.remove('blurred-balance');
            balanceElement.classList.add('visible');
            localStorage.setItem('privacyMode', 'disabled');
        } else {
            balanceElement.classList.add('blurred-balance');
            balanceElement.classList.remove('visible');
            localStorage.setItem('privacyMode', 'enabled');
        }
    }

    // Check the privacy mode status on page load
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
        // The balance is only rendered for a signed-in user, but this script is
        // on every page. Without this the handler threw on every logged-out
        // page - login and register included - and took the rest of itself with
        // it while filling the console of anyone looking for a real error.
        var balanceElement = document.getElementById('balanceShowHeader1');
        if (!balanceElement) return;

        if (localStorage.getItem('privacyMode') === 'enabled') {
            balanceElement.classList.add('blurred-balance');
        } else {
            balanceElement.classList.remove('blurred-balance');
            balanceElement.classList.add('visible');
        }
    });
</script>
<!-- end header -->


<script type="85cd9de20ab38d2dba3dc7e1-text/javascript">
    (function () {
        var header = document.getElementById('top_header');
        if (!header) return;

        function reserve() {
            document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
                '--header-height', Math.ceil(header.getBoundingClientRect().height) + 'px'
            );
        }

        // Runs at parse time, before first paint. getBoundingClientRect forces
        // layout, so this is correct even in a background tab - which matters,
        // because the two watchers below are driven by the rendering pipeline
        // and do not run until the tab is actually painted.
        reserve();

        // Rotation and window resizing, and the case where ResizeObserver is
        // unavailable. Cheap, and not conditional on the observer.
        window.addEventListener('resize', reserve);

        // Anything that changes the bar's height without changing the window's:
        // the banner wrapping, a notice being added to it.
        if (window.ResizeObserver) new ResizeObserver(reserve).observe(header);

        // Webfonts land after this runs and can retype the banner onto a
        // second line.
        if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(reserve);
    })();
</script>

<script type="85cd9de20ab38d2dba3dc7e1-text/javascript">
    (function () {
        var sheet = document.getElementById('categorySheet');
        var backdrop = document.querySelector('.bottom-sheet__backdrop');
        if (!sheet || !backdrop) return;

        var opener = null;

        function open() {
            opener = document.activeElement;
            sheet.hidden = false;
            backdrop.hidden = false;
            // Let the browser paint the hidden state first, or the transition
            // is skipped and the sheet appears rather than slides.
            requestAnimationFrame(function () {
                sheet.classList.add('bottom-sheet--open');
                backdrop.classList.add('bottom-sheet__backdrop--open');
            });
            document.body.classList.add('sheet-open');
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-bottom-sheet-open]').forEach(function (b) {
                b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
            });
            var first = sheet.querySelector('.bottom-sheet__close');
            if (first) first.focus();
        }

        function close() {
            sheet.classList.remove('bottom-sheet--open');
            backdrop.classList.remove('bottom-sheet__backdrop--open');
            document.body.classList.remove('sheet-open');
            document.querySelectorAll('[data-bottom-sheet-open]').forEach(function (b) {
                b.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
            });
            window.setTimeout(function () {
                sheet.hidden = true;
                backdrop.hidden = true;
            }, 280);
            if (opener && opener.focus) opener.focus();
        }

        document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
            if (e.target.closest('[data-bottom-sheet-open]')) { e.preventDefault(); open(); }
            else if (e.target.closest('[data-bottom-sheet-close]')) { e.preventDefault(); close(); }
        });

        document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
            if (e.key === 'Escape' && !sheet.hidden) close();
        });
    })();
</script>

    <script type="85cd9de20ab38d2dba3dc7e1-text/javascript">

                    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
                function shouldShowAlert() {
                    const lastShown = localStorage.getItem('scam_alert_last_shown_ad');
                    if (!lastShown) {
                        return true;
                    }
                    const lastShownTime = new Date(lastShown);
                    const currentTime = new Date();
                    const hoursDiff = (currentTime - lastShownTime) / (1000 * 60 * 60);
                    return hoursDiff >= 12;
                }

                function setAlertShown() {
                    localStorage.setItem('scam_alert_last_shown_ad', new Date().toISOString());
                }

                if (shouldShowAlert()) {
                    window.pauseNotifications = true;

                    // Swal.fire({
                    //     imageUrl: 'https://usr.dokan-cdn.com/scam-alert-new.png',
                    //     imageAlt: 'Scam Alert',
                    //     confirmButtonText: "انا على علم",
                    //     allowEscapeKey: false,
                    //     allowOutsideClick: false
                    // }).then((result) => {
                    //     if (result.isConfirmed) {
                    //         setAlertShown();
                    //     }
                    // });
                    //
                    Swal.fire({
                        imageUrl: 'https://usr.dokan-cdn.com/telegram-ad.png',
                        imageAlt: 'Scam Alert',
                        confirmButtonText: "تمام توكل على الله",
                        allowEscapeKey: false,
                        html: `
                                    <a style="font-weight: bold; color: #6164ff" target="_blank" class="btn btn-info" href="https://t.me/USR_GG">دخول قناة التيلي جرام</a>

                        `,
                        allowOutsideClick: false
                    }).then((result) => {
                        if (result.isConfirmed) {
                            setAlertShown();
                            window.pauseNotifications = false;
                        }
                    });
                }
            });

            
                    



        function banItem(type,id){
            $('.blockButton').hide();

            Swal.fire({
                title: 'هل أنت متأكد من حظر المستخدم ' ,
                text: "لن يتمكن المستخدم من التواصل معك أو الشراء منك ولن تتمكن من التواصل معه أو الشراء منه",
                icon: 'info',
                showCancelButton: true,
                confirmButtonColor: '#3085d6',
                cancelButtonColor: '#6b7280',
                cancelButtonText: 'إلغاء',
                confirmButtonText: 'Yes, block'
            }).then((result) => {
                if (result.isConfirmed) {
                    axios.post("https://usr.gg/blocks",{
                        type,
                        id
                    }).catch( err => {
                        alert(err?.response?.data?.message)
                        $('.blockButton').show();
                    })


                }else{
                    $('.blockButton').show();
                }
            })
        }

<script type="85cd9de20ab38d2dba3dc7e1-text/javascript">
(function () {
    var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="usr-view"]');
    if (!meta || !meta.content) return;

    var parts = meta.content.split(':');
    if (parts.length !== 2 || !parts[1]) return;

    var token = document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]');
    if (!token) return;

    var sent = false;

    function send() {
        if (sent) return;
        sent = true;

        try {
            fetch('/api/analytics/collect', {
                method: 'POST',
                // The session cookie is what attributes the view to a person.
                credentials: 'same-origin',
                // Survives the user navigating away straight after landing.
                keepalive: true,
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    'X-CSRF-TOKEN': token.content,
                    'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest'
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    type: parts[0],
                    id: parts[1],
                    path: location.pathname,
                    url: location.href.slice(0, 512)
                })
            }).catch(function () {
                // A view counter is decoration. It never gets to break a page.
            });
        } catch (e) {}
    }

    if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
        send();
    } else {
        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function onShow() {
            if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') {
                document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onShow);
                send();
            }
        });
    }
})();
</script>


<script type="85cd9de20ab38d2dba3dc7e1-text/javascript">
window.USRCart = (function () {
    function applyCount(count) {
        if (isNaN(count)) return;

        var header = document.getElementById('usr_cart_header_counter');
        if (header) header.textContent = count;

        var badge = document.getElementById('usr_cart_tab_badge');
        if (!badge) return;

        badge.textContent = count > 99 ? '99+' : count;
        badge.hidden = count < 1;
        // Restart the pop rather than let a second add find the class already
        // applied and do nothing.
        badge.classList.remove('bottom-nav__badge--pop');
        void badge.offsetWidth;
        badge.classList.add('bottom-nav__badge--pop');
    }

    function confirmOn(btn) {
        if (!btn) return;
        btn.classList.remove('is-added');
        void btn.offsetWidth;
        btn.classList.add('is-added');
        window.setTimeout(function () { btn.classList.remove('is-added'); }, 1400);
    }

    return {
        applyCount: applyCount,
        confirmOn: confirmOn,
        // The whole confirmation, for callers that have the raw response body.
        added: function (responseBody, btn) {
            applyCount(parseInt(String(responseBody).trim(), 10));
            confirmOn(btn);
            if (window.ShowSuccess) ShowSuccess('Added to your cart');
        },
        failed: function () {
            if (window.ShowError) ShowError('Could not add to your cart, please try again');
        }
    };
})();

(function () {
    var isCartPost = function (el) {
        return el && el.getAttribute && (el.getAttribute('hx-post') || '') === '/cart';
    };

    document.body.addEventListener('htmx:beforeRequest', function (e) {
        if (!isCartPost(e.detail.elt)) return;
        e.detail.elt.classList.add('is-adding');
    });

    document.body.addEventListener('htmx:afterRequest', function (e) {
        var btn = e.detail.elt;
        if (!isCartPost(btn)) return;

        btn.classList.remove('is-adding');

        var xhr = e.detail.xhr;
        if (!e.detail.successful || !xhr || xhr.status >= 400) {
            window.USRCart.failed();
            return;
        }

        window.USRCart.added(xhr.responseText, btn);
    });
})();
</script>


<script type="85cd9de20ab38d2dba3dc7e1-text/javascript">
(function () {
    var EYE = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 12s3.5-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-3.5 7-10 7-10-7-10-7Z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/></svg>';
    var EYE_OFF = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M9.9 4.24A9.1 9.1 0 0 1 12 4c6.5 0 10 7 10 7a17.3 17.3 0 0 1-2.2 3.15M6.6 6.6A17.3 17.3 0 0 0 2 11s3.5 7 10 7a9.1 9.1 0 0 0 4-.9"/><path d="m2 2 20 20"/></svg>';

    var SHOW = "Show password";
    var HIDE = "Hide password";

    function attach(input) {
        if (input.dataset.usrSecretReady) return;
        input.dataset.usrSecretReady = '1';

        var wrap = document.createElement('span');
        wrap.className = 'usr-secret-wrap';
        input.parentNode.insertBefore(wrap, input);
        wrap.appendChild(input);

        var btn = document.createElement('button');
        btn.type = 'button';                 // never submit the form it sits in
        btn.className = 'usr-secret-reveal';
        btn.innerHTML = EYE;
        btn.setAttribute('aria-label', SHOW);
        btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false');

        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
            var shown = input.type === 'text';
            input.type = shown ? 'password' : 'text';
            btn.innerHTML = shown ? EYE : EYE_OFF;
            btn.setAttribute('aria-label', shown ? SHOW : HIDE);
            btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', shown ? 'false' : 'true');
        });

        wrap.appendChild(btn);
    }

    function scan(root) {
        (root || document).querySelectorAll('input.usr-secret').forEach(attach);
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () { scan(); });
    } else {
        scan();
    }

    // Fields added after load must get a control too, and they arrive by more
    // than one route: htmx swaps on some pages, and on sell.blade.php the
    // replacement-account rows are built from a JS template string and inserted
    // with jQuery. Watching the tree covers every case without this needing to
    // know which mechanism inserted what.
    if ('MutationObserver' in window) {
        new MutationObserver(function (records) {
            for (var i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
                var added = records[i].addedNodes;
                for (var j = 0; j < added.length; j++) {
                    var node = added[j];
                    if (node.nodeType !== 1) continue;
                    if (node.matches && node.matches('input.usr-secret')) attach(node);
                    scan(node);
                }
            }
        }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });
    }

    window.USRSecretFields = { scan: scan };
})();
</script>
